if test -z "$objects"; then				\
  echo 'int __libgcc_eh_dummy;' > eh_dummy.c;		\
  aarch64-none-elf-gcc -g -O2 -O2  -g -O2 -fPIC -march=armv8-a+crc+crypto -mtune=cortex-a57 -mtp=soft -DFNX_CORTEX_A57_ONLY -DIN_GCC  -g -DIN_LIBGCC2 -fbuilding-libgcc -fno-stack-protector  -Werror -Wno-prio-ctor-dtor  -I. -I. -I../gcc -I../../fnx016-gcc-source/gcc-16.1.0/libgcc -I../../fnx016-gcc-source/gcc-16.1.0/libgcc/. -I../../fnx016-gcc-source/gcc-16.1.0/libgcc/../gcc -I../../fnx016-gcc-source/gcc-16.1.0/libgcc/../include -I../../fnx016-gcc-source/gcc-16.1.0/libgcc/config/aarch64 -I../../fnx016-gcc-source/gcc-16.1.0/libgcc/config/libbid -DENABLE_DECIMAL_BID_FORMAT -DHAVE_CC_TLS   -fvisibility=hidden -DHIDE_EXPORTS -c eh_dummy.c		\
     -o eh_dummy.o;				\
  objects=eh_dummy.o;				\
fi;							\
aarch64-none-elf-ar  rc libgcc.a $objects
aarch64-none-elf-ranlib libgcc.a
